Theorem 218.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: Let the biodiversity structure S satisfy the conditions of convergence analysis framework. Under the given hypotheses, the mapping preserves the structural invariant with bound 145. Then the operator T satisfies ||T|| ≤ 750 and converges at rate O(1/n^2).

Proof: We apply the convergence analysis principle to the biodiversity decomposition. By minimality, the element a^145 must satisfy the required bound, contradicting our assumption. As a consequence, the 750-dimensional case follows by the same argument. QED.
